3315 — full maltese

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3315 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

An advanced gymnastics hold on rings with the arms out wide and the body straight. It demands huge strength in the chest, shoulders and core.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Start by standing with your feet shoulder-width apart and your arms extended straight out to the sides.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Start by standing with your feet shoulder-width apart and your arms extended straight out to the sides. 3315.json#/instructions/0
  2. Slowly lean forward, keeping your arms straight, until your upper body is parallel to the ground. 3315.json#/instructions/1
  3. Engage your core and hold this position for a few seconds. 3315.json#/instructions/2
  4. Return to the starting position by pushing through your feet and standing back up. 3315.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3315.json#/instructions/4

3316 — barbell weightlifting complex

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3316 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A flowing series of barbell lifts done back to back without putting the bar down. It works your whole body and your conditioning.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Load a light to moderate barbell and stand over it with feet hip width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3316.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Load a light to moderate barbell and stand over it with feet hip width apart. 3316.json#/instructions/0
  2. Perform the first lift of your chosen sequence, such as a deadlift. 3316.json#/instructions/1
  3. Move straight into the next lift without setting the bar down. 3316.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep each rep crisp with a braced core and a flat back. 3316.json#/instructions/3
  5. Finish all lifts for one round, then lower the bar and rest before repeating. 3316.json#/instructions/4

Proposed staging

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Squat low at the edge of the vehicle with a flat back and hands underneath. 3317.json#/instructions/0
  2. Brace your core and pull your chest up. 3317.json#/instructions/1
  3. Drive hard through your legs and lift the edge to hip height. 3317.json#/instructions/2
  4. Shift your hands and push up and over with your legs and arms. 3317.json#/instructions/3
  5. Let it roll over fully, then step back and reset for the next flip. 3317.json#/instructions/4

3318 — swing 360

3319 — dumbbell lying on floor hammer press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3319 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supported_lying.

A floor press with palms facing each other, which is kind to your shoulders. It trains the chest and triceps through a short range.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Lie on the floor with knees bent and a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing in. Start with your upper arms resting on the floor.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie on the floor with knees bent and a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing in. 3319.json#/instructions/0
  2. Start with your upper arms resting on the floor. 3319.json#/instructions/1
  3. Press the dumbbells straight up over your chest. 3319.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause briefly at the top without locking out hard. 3319.json#/instructions/3
  5. Lower slowly until your triceps touch the floor and repeat. 3319.json#/instructions/4
